## 2026-05-29 14:50 UTC | RESEARCH / TAKER_FADE | "Screw the takers" thesis FALSIFIED on weather — market too efficient, flow informed, edges don't persist | Pivoted from MM fingerprint to reverse-engineering TAKERS (data-api /trades.proxyWallet=taker, carries outcome/side/name). Harvested 458k trades over 4 days (05-15 + 05-24..26), 2300+ resolved temp markets → data/taker_trades_*.parquet. Tools: analysis/weather/taker_{harvest,profile,evmap,edge_validate,flow_test}.py. FINDINGS (all per-MARKET to kill pseudo-replication): (1) Per-TRADE EV map showed huge apparent edges (buy YES 0.5-0.8 +13-16¢/sh) that VANISHED per-market — pseudo-replication + a few concentrated markets. (2) Market is EFFICIENTLY priced: corr(vwap,resolution)=0.93-0.95, Brier 0.011-0.015; bimodal/near-deterministic (contested 0.4-0.6 middle is nearly empty). (3) Taker flow is INFORMED not noise: corr(flow,surprise)=+0.23..+0.32 → follow not fade. (4) DECISIVE: wallet skill/dumbness does NOT persist across windows — corr(edge_per_$ A,B)=-0.068, corr(PnL A,B)=-0.088 → can't target "known losers". (5) Only per-market-significant price cell OOS: BUY YES 0.5-0.8 = -0.095 (favorites mildly OVERpriced) but small-capacity. (6) Last sliver: fading the 22 persistent-prey = +0.022/sh GROSS [+0.006,+0.038] per-market, but eaten by the 1-3¢ entry spread → net ~0; capacity ~$1.4k/day gross split among all faders. VERDICT: no fat fade-the-takers goldmine on weather; the only real edge remains SELECTIVE lockout/tail trading (provenance-gated, crowded). Other verticals (sports/politics) untested but same structural priors (efficient, non-persistent) caution against. Memory: [[project_taker_fade_falsified]].

## 2026-05-29 14:45 UTC | RESEARCH / LOCKOUT (fold-in from MM fingerprint) | Staleness dynamics: lockout-NO edge is LOW-CAPACITY, LATE-window, and pre-fix early edge was a CONTAMINATION artifact | lockout_staleness.py (analysis/weather/) — companion to lockout_capacity.py (which only saw FIRST capture). Used all 51.8k ~60s snapshots across 598 locked (token,day) events, time-axis = seconds_since_first_lockout, FILLABLE NO asks only (real no_book/no_ask_clob; NOT the 1-yes_bid fallback that inflated prior "edge"). FINDINGS: (1) Only 28% of locked events (32% post-fix) EVER offer a fillable cheap NO (edge≥1%) — most lockouts have NO correctly priced ≥0.99, nothing cheap to buy → capacity is small (consistent w/ rare-but-high-WR). (2) On clean POST-FIX lockouts, fillable cheap NO is ~absent in the first hour (med_depth $0, %edge≥1% ~25-37%) and only materializes >60min in (edge 0.13, depth $49, 82% edge≥1%) → edge is NOT a be-fast race; it's late-window/patient. (3) PRE-FIX showed big EARLY cheap-NO (0-30s: edge 0.13, $62 depth) that VANISHES post-fix → that early edge was largely FALSE lockouts (asos 1-min spikes); corroborates M1β provenance fix as load-bearing, don't trust pre-fix lockout PnL. (4) Fingerprint↔lockout synergy WEAKER than hoped: tail makers sell at 0.998+ (not the ~0.87 cheap-NO seller), so they are NOT the lockout-NO counterparty — they provide stale YES-BID exit liquidity (0.001-0.03), capturing which as NO needs a mint primitive we lack. CAVEAT: >60min cheap-NO population may be concentrated in few buckets — needs lockout_resolution_join validation before any sizing claim. Output: analysis/weather/lockout_staleness_out.txt.

## 2026-05-29 14:25 UTC | RESEARCH / MM_FINGERPRINT | Behavioral fingerprint: determinism EXTREME but boundary-pinned → meta-game collapses into existing lockout-NO edge | mm_fingerprint.py (analysis/weather/). Decoded OrderFilled DATA (makerAssetId/amounts) → per-maker price/side/token; 317 maker-fills attributed to the 31 targets. fill price = maker's exact posted limit price (validated: complementary mint pair sums to 1.00). Determinism (25 makers ≥5 fills): median top-3-price concentration = 1.00, median price std = 0.001, tail_share ≈ 1.00 for ~22/31, 19/25 one-sided. TWO SPECIES: (A) ~20 deep-tail vacuum cleaners pinned at 0.001 (bid dead tails) or 0.998-0.999 (buy/sell near-certain) — maximally deterministic but pinned at the tick BOUNDARY, so "rest at the bot's destination before it moves" does NOT apply; (B) ~4 mid-book cent-tick MMs quoting across a range (0x26123cbf p̄0.13 std0.22 103s; 0xc34f6b08 p̄0.46 no-tails) — the only place quote-prediction could live, but thin (4-16 fills). VERDICT: determinism confirmed, but Species-A slow tail makers ARE the same counterparties the settlement-lock/LOCKOUT-NO edge already harvests (94.6% WR) — the "trade the maker not the weather" meta-game largely COLLAPSES INTO the existing lockout edge + a thin mid-book sliver; not a distinct new edge. Independent confirmation that lockout counterparties are genuinely slow. CAVEAT: token→YES/NO/city map mostly missed (only 1194 logged ids vs current markets) so can't confirm tail makers are NO-side specifically; cadence sampling-limited. Output: analysis/weather/mm_fingerprint_out.json.

## 2026-05-28 16:00 UTC | STWA | 12 structural fixes deployed in one session — model and execution overhaul | (1) YES/NO pool split in allocation: horse-race Kelly (mutually exclusive) for YES buckets, independent Kelly for NO buckets — old single-pool was hitting p_sum>>1 for NO bets with p_win≈1, collapsing all stakes to stake_min. (2) 51-city expansion: 15 new Polymarket cities added (Ankara→Zhengzhou) to stwa_params + CITY_PEAK_HOUR_UTC + ICAO_TO_SLUG, calibrated from 2023-2024 NWP archive. (3) NWP bias recalibrated for all 51 cities from 2023-2025 ASOS vs GFS (mean abs error 0.61→0.03°C). city_order updated to 51 (new cities were silently excluded from Kalman joint state). (4) 2D Langevin velocity state: dX=V dt, dV=(−γV−κX)dt+σdW replaces 1D OU. Velocity estimated from OLS on 6-point raw-residual buffer; exact F via scipy.linalg.expm + Van Loan Q. Running_max-consistent initial condition: x_hat_eff=max(x_hat, M0−NWP_biased) when running_max_ts<1h. (5) POST_PEAK override tightened: only fires when last_temp within 0.5°C of running_max — prevents Milan/Helsinki pattern of entering high-bucket signals while temperature is already declining from peak. (6) Running_max monotone fix: cs.running_max = max(cs.running_max, nms_running_max, temp_c) — NWP feed for RKPK was sending decreasing running_max values (27→26→25→23), causing engine to reset its floor and generate wrong signals (Busan NO [26.5,27.5] entered with M0=23 when actual max was 27). (7) Kalman state persistence: X, P, per-city velocity state saved every 20 updates to data/stwa_kalman_state.npz + stwa_city_state.json; restored on restart — eliminates 60-min warm-up blind window. (8) NEG_RISK_ARB fully fixed: 3 silent kill points removed in execution loop (live_ask<0.005 was blocking cheap YES tokens; edge check was killing low-p arb buckets; _fired_tokens seeded from open_positions was blocking already-held YES tokens). Arb now uses NEG_RISK_ARB_MIN=$0.50 stake (bypasses stake_min=$3 inflation). (9) Opposite-direction dedup: before entering regular signal, check if any token in same market is in open_positions — prevents YES+NO on same bucket. (10) NEG_RISK_ARB depth check: k clamped so k×ask_i ≤ usd_depth per bucket.

## 2026-05-23 06:XX UTC | BUG / WEATHER | _outcome_prob unit-blind ±0.5°C pad fixed across all weather strategies | _outcome_prob hardcoded ±0.5°C as the bucket rounding pad. _parse_outcome returns lo/hi in °C regardless of native unit, so the ±0.5°C was applied unconditionally — over-padding F-bucket markets by 0.45°C (treating a 1.11°C/2°F bucket as 1.56°C wide) and inflating fair_prob ~30–40%. Empirical proof: Chicago May 22 WU displayed integer 65°F from raw 64.94°F, resolved YES on "64-65°F" bucket → confirms whole-degree resolution; SF 69.80°F→68°F→"66°F or higher" YES; Dallas 87.80°F→87°F→"80°F or higher" YES. Fix: moved unit-aware padding into _parse_outcome (±0.5°F for F-range/F-bounded, ±0.5°C for C-range/C-bounded), removed pad from _outcome_prob; also removed the double-pad at METAR-status (lines 2259-2260) and INTRADAY hi_bound (line 2681). BRACKET shadow re-audit (n=6 events today): old combined_edge +0.19–+0.59 vs corrected +0.007 to +0.362; 4 of 6 still pass BRACKET_COMBINED_FAIR_MIN=0.55. STRAT_1 impact: Chicago μ=21.1, σ=1.32, "70-71°F" → fair drops 0.436 → 0.319 (now fails MIN_FAIR_PROB=0.45). 7/7 parse unit tests + full Chicago bucket grid Σ=1.000000 (mass conservation proven). All 6 _outcome_prob call sites (STRAT_1 1586, BRACKET 1485+, METAR 2278/2286, EXIT_EVAL 2402, bucket-switch 2512, INTRADAY 2692) consistent under fix.

## 2026-05-20 23:XX UTC | STRATEGY / WEATHER | FINAL: Comprehensive backtest validates WEATHER_REPRICING (repricing-first, dual-path) | Comprehensive backtest on 9 historical markets with all 7 models (AROME, DWD, ECMWF, GFS, JMA, CMA, BOM) validates repricing-first strategy. ## 2026-05-20 23:XX UTC | STRATEGY / WEATHER | Dual-strategy analysis: Repricing wins 90.6% over resolution betting | Analyzed two trading approaches on 9 mispriced markets: (A) Temporary Repricing (buy at entry, exit at repricing to fair value) vs (B) Resolution Betting (buy at entry, hold to resolution). Key insight: repricing happens ~100% of time in sample (8/8), but resolution forecast accuracy only 50% (4/8). Entry: Detect >8% mispricings via fair_value from ECMWF. Exit: Monitor bid every 10s, exit at 90% of fair_value. Timeout: 240s safety exit. Expected: 90-100% WR, +40-60% avg ROI per trade. Phase 1C (revised): repricing detection + exit logic (Week 1), shadow test (Week 2-3), deploy if validated >80% WR (Week 3+). ## 2026-05-11 18:50 UTC | KILL / ORACLE_SWEEP | Oracle_sweep disabled — structural flaw confirmed, all positions lost | Root cause: (1) A1 flat-entry fires on 15m windows using 5m momentum signal (feed._spot_open_5m), not the 15m candle. At 15:30-15:45 UTC, BTC -0.136% (15m DOWN) but A1 saw +0.017% 5m momentum and bought UP. (2) A2/A3 sweep: stale cheap asks at T+0 exist only on the LOSING side — MMs cancel winning-side asks before window close. The strategy systematically buys the side the market already priced as loser. Near-flat candles (-0.0002%) show DOWN token at 0.02 (2% probability) = market knew UP would win via Chainlink. Total damage: $487 spent today, all unredeemable (not stale MM errors, genuine losers). Redeemer curPrice filter also fixed (removed `curPrice>=0.95` check — settled tokens always show curPrice=0), interval 60s. Oracle_sweep structurally unsound — do not re-enable without proof that cheap asks survive on winner side.

## 2026-05-11 21:00 UTC | RESEARCH / EXPLOIT | Pre-open market creation exploit investigation — findings | Investigated 'bot buying at 0.01 before market opened' on non-crypto markets. Full lifecycle mapped: (1) Markets pre-created 24h before window. (2) Immediately after creation (~15-60s), CLOB REST /book shows seed: ASK=0.99×10, BID=0.01×10 — NOT cheap. (3) Within 60s, MM seeds at 0.50/0.50 and REST /book returns 404 (WS-only from that point). (4) No cheap ASK < 0.20 observed in any state. Non-crypto markets (LoL etc.) have same structure with slower seed growth (10→551 shares over 2h). Possible explanations for observed bot: (a) inverted seed bug now patched — initial ASK was at 0.01 before correction; (b) different market type (old FPMM AMM, no longer used); (c) CTF direct split + CLOB dump at wrong price. Built analytics/market_creation_monitor.py. VERDICT: No evidence of current exploit. Monitor must run hours to catch new batches. Capital needed before any live sniping.

2026-05-28 21:30 UTC | STWA / P1 BUNDLE | math hardening — bias hour-varying, OU MoM-fitted, phase-c_age, Kelly-gate | (P1-4) live NWP anchor now uses per-(month,hour) bias (was hour=0 only); previous code collapsed 1-2°C diurnal bias variation. (P1-1) OU κ/σ refit via method-of-moments (κ=−ln(ρ̂), σ²=2κ·V̂) instead of Nelder-Mead MLE; MLE was biased high by heavy-tailed (kurt+1.7) non-Gaussian residuals; new fit also separates within-day adjacent pairs from cross-day false-adjacent pairs in lag-1 computation. Tokyo bin 0 κ: 0.222 (old MLE) → 0.248 (new MoM). Bias now empirical-Bayes shrunk: w=n/(n+30), so US cities (124 obs/cell) get ~80% per-hour, non-US (28 obs/cell) get ~48% per-hour mix with month mean. MIN_OBS 200→20 + shrinkage replaces hard fallback. Non-US ECMWF coverage gap on Open-Meteo confirmed (2021-2023 NaN, only 2024 has data — ~28 obs/cell vs US 124/cell). Jinan re-inserted post-fit (ASOS 851 rows < 1000 fit floor). (P1-8) c_age decay τ now phase-dependent: PRE_PEAK 90min, AT_PEAK 15min, POST_PEAK 120min (was uniform 0.15 decay/hr). (P1-9) edge gate replaced flat EDGE_MIN=0.08 with composite EDGE_MIN=0.04 AND Kelly-f* > 0.015. Scales naturally: high-ask sells fire on narrow edges, low-ask longshots need bigger edge.

Tail mass at 4σ: 25× higher than Gaussian. Cheap-tail bucket probabilities will rise 2-5×. (P2-I) Added GEV closed-form bucket prob path: per (city, month) fit of GEV(loc, scale, shape) on daily-max residuals using scipy.genextreme. Stored in stwa_params.json. Engine computes both MC and GEV bucket probs; logs both in shadow Signal for A/B comparison over coming days. Initial cross-check: Atlanta May peak h hour MC and GEV agree within 1pp. (P2-3) Spatial covariance is now LW + 35% physical kernel (great-circle exp(-d/800km) × peak-hour-phase Gaussian). Regularizes spurious anti-correlations (Jeddah-Tel Aviv was -0.47, now -0.04). Mean off-diag corr stable at 0.016, max correlations preserved (Austin-Denver 0.41). (P2-VI) Persistent drift baseline: per (city, hour_utc) EMA of recent residuals with τ=30 days. Catches climate drift + NWP-version updates. Updates each METAR (α=1-exp(-Δt_days/30)). Subtracted from forecast μ in both Kalman update and MC path generation. Persisted in stwa_city_state.json. Bounded ±3°C. fit_params re-run; jinan re-inserted with Gumbel fallback (n=0 GEV) + physical-kernel cross-correlations.

2026-05-29 04:11 UTC | STWA / P3 ORACLE AUDIT | running_max contaminated by sub-hourly point obs — M1β bug re-introduced inside engine | Oracle audit found STWA's cs.running_max was lifted above the official METAR high the WU/PM oracle resolves against. ROOT CAUSE: on_metar folded the raw point obs into running_max via `_new_max(running_max, temp_c)` (stwa_engine.py:418 no-NWP branch, :511 Kalman branch). On the NMS path temp_c is the latest sub-hourly Synoptic 1-min / JMA 10-min reading — which the oracle never sees. The NMS caller already passes official_running_max_c (M1β fix), but the engine overrode it by maxing in temp_c. Separately the AWC caller (weather_arb.py:3616) passed the all-source new_max, not official_running_max_c. BLAST RADIUS: running_max feeds (a) the <30min-to-close certainty rule (pins p=1.0 to the bucket holding running_max, stwa_engine.py:562) and (b) the path initial-condition + path-max floor M0 (stwa_engine.py:590,603) — so a spurious high running_max mis-prices entries and can pin certainty to a bucket the official high won't reach. 12 of 51 STWA cities are on sub-hourly feeds (KLGA/KORD/KLAX/KMIA/KSFO/RJTT/KDAL/KHOU/KSEA/KBKF/KATL/KAUS — the highest-liquidity US markets + Tokyo). FIX: engine trusts the caller's running_max arg, maxed only against its own prior cs.running_max for monotonic safety, never against temp_c; AWC caller now passes official_running_max_c or new_max (matches NMS caller). last_temp still uses sub-hourly obs (fine — it's a nowcast input, not a resolution input). 2026-05-29 05:10 UTC | STWA / TIER-1 ARB MARGIN | multi-leg neg-risk arb requires wider Σask margin (≥4 legs → 0.80, was flat 0.85) | Tier-1 of the 4-agent math rebuild. VERIFICATION FINDING: the other two proposed Tier-1 items were already correctly handled in-code and NOT changed — (a) impossible buckets are masked via the M0 floor (path_max=np.maximum(M0, T_paths.max), so hi≤M0 ⇒ prob 0); (b) per-cycle bucket probs are over disjoint buckets so already sum ≤1, and the arb Σp_model≥0.95 exhaustivity gate is robust to the per-bucket miscalibration because Σp only measures range-coverage not within-range allocation. 2026-05-29 05:45 UTC | STWA / TIER-2 PEAK-ANCHORED PRICER (SHADOW) | new daily-max pricer logged for calibration A/B vs MC/GEV — NOT live | Tier-2 of the math rebuild addresses the root cause of the 4.3× YES overconfidence: the MC takes path_max over the WHOLE remaining day, over-spreading the daily-max distribution. New _peak_anchored_bucket_probs: daily_max ≈ μ_peak + Y(t*), Y(t*)~N(m*,s*²) where (m*,s*²) are the residual posterior propagated to the peak hour via the SAME 2D transition the MC uses (deterministic moment propagation, reuses _vel_step + _step_cache). s* stays bounded near stationary residual std (σ²/2γκ) instead of inflating with horizon. Single Gaussian CDF F_S(b)=Φ((b−μ_peak−m*)/s*) → coherent (Σ=1 by construction), with hard running-max floor F_M=1[x≥M0]·F_S. NOTE: I did NOT implement agent C's literal closed form — its √(2πv/|g''|) window constant is dimensionally inconsistent (v/|g''| is °C·h², not h²). First tried a Rice/Poisson up-crossing integral but it mis-handles the DETERMINISTIC diurnal sweep (under-counts the certain crossing of levels on the way up to the peak → non-monotone F_S, Σ>1); replaced with the clean peak-value Gaussian (sup-correction omitted as a fragile 2nd-order tail effect, deferred). Validated numerically: coherent Σ=1, masks impossible buckets, shifts with m*. SHADOW ONLY: _forecast_bucket_probs still returns MC probs to the allocator; PA stashed on cs.pa_probs_last and logged per-bucket to logs/shadow/hot/<date>/stwa_pricer_eval.jsonl (MC vs GEV vs PA, for EVERY priced bucket incl. suspended-YES side). Validate calibration vs ASOS over coming days before making PA primary. Commit pending.

2026-05-29 06:30 UTC | STWA / TIER-3 JOINT 2N KALMAN (SHADOW) | joint position-velocity filter runs parallel to live, logged for A/B — NOT live | Tier-3 of the math rebuild. Replaces (in SHADOW) the incoherent split of position-only OU Kalman + OLS-on-residuals velocity with a single joint (X,V) 2N state consistent with the inertial-Langevin the MC simulator already assumes. SDE ds=A s dt+dW, A=[[0,I],[−diag(κ),−γI]]; F=expm(AΔt), Q via Van Loan (4N×4N=204²), cross-city noise C_σ_ij=2γ√(κ_iκ_j)·C_ij (NOTE: agent B's two C_σ formulas were inconsistent on the diagonal; this form gives stationary Var(X)=C exactly — verified). Joseph-form update, separate _shadow_lock so it never blocks the live update path. (F,Q) cached by (6h κ-bin, round(Δt,2)): cache-miss 180ms, cache-hit 12ms — warms to ~20 keys/day. Offline-validated: PSD-stable over 200 steps, stationary pos-var→diag(C), vel-var→κ·C. KEY FINDING from the offline test: under the hard-coded γ=1.5, the joint velocity is OVER-DAMPED (decorrelates in ~0.67h < hourly obs cadence) → it produces a near-zero/noisy velocity that does NOT beat the model-free OLS for trend-tracking (warming test: OLS v=+0.32 correct sign; joint v=−0.12 ≈0). This CONFIRMS agent B's recommendation that γ must be FIT per city, not fixed. So the real Tier-3 unlock is the joint (κ,γ,σ) MLE refit (a calibration task), which this shadow infra will then validate. SHADOW ONLY: live state (self._X/_P + OLS v_hat) untouched and still drives arb/NO; joint estimates logged per-city in stwa_state.jsonl (x_hat_joint, v_hat_joint, pv_var_joint vs v_hat_ols/pv_var_ols). Do NOT promote joint over live until γ is fit AND shadow shows it wins. Commit pending.

## 2026-06-04 | SYSTEM/WEATHER — (1) FAVYES daily cap UNCAPPED, (2) sizing fires off LIVE USDC, (3) 0.99 early-exit on all held-to-resolution weather positions (user-directed) | strategy/weather_arb.py | THREE user-directed changes. (1) UNCAP FAVYES: FAVYES_MAX_DAILY_FIRES 50→1e9 (effectively uncapped); real available USDC is now the only cap. Also moved the per-token dedup (_favyes_fired / _favyes_fires_today) to fire ONLY on a confirmed FILL (was: counted every attempt incl no-fills) — so a favorite that no-filled for lack of cash retries on a later cycle once cash frees up (manual sells / 0.99 exits). (2) FIRE-ON-AVAILABLE-USDC: _get_bankroll() (the free-cash basis for ALL weather sizing/budget) now prefers the LIVE CLOB USDC balance via new _free_usdc_cached() (fetch_usdc_balance, cached 45s; None→falls back to capital−held). Rationale: user sells manually between the 5-min reconciles, so capital−held understates real free cash; live USDC == free deployable cash and reflects manual sells + deposits immediately. Risk-of-ruin floor still uses bankroll.capital (total equity) — unchanged. (3) 0.99 EXIT: new _exit_at_099() runs in the metar slow-path (~60s); for every held-to-resolution class (_STWA_RESOLVE_CLASSES: FAVYES/STWA/M1β/OFI/FADE) it polls the book and, when a ≥0.99 bid with depth ≥ floor(shares) exists, taker-sells whole shares at the bid then close_position() INLINE. Captures near-certain winners ~1¢ early + recycles capital into new fires; hold-to-resolution stays the fallback. Implemented as poll-then-taker (NOT a resting limit order) because (a) a resting sell fills "externally" → the bot wouldn't book it → _stwa_close_resolved books from the Gamma outcome with NO balance check → DOUBLE-COUNT; selling+close_position in one step books PnL exactly once and removes the token so the settler can't re-book it (same discipline as _ofi_manage_exits); (b) orders.limit_sell DOES NOT EXIST. Flooring to whole shares (0.99 forces whole-share CLOB ticks) keeps the full-fill check exact (no partial-sell orphan); sub-1-share remainder is dust that redeems at resolution. Constants: EXIT099_ENABLED/PRICE/MIN_SHARES. ⚠ DISCOVERED LATENT BUG (not fixed, flagged): OrderManager has NO limit_sell method, yet 7 live exit call-sites use it (M1β TP @0.999, OFI decay/scalp/timecap, NOWCAST collapse salvage, nwp_lag) — all throw AttributeError swallowed by try/except → every weather early-exit is silently DEAD; everything has been holding to resolution. _exit_at_099 deliberately uses the working _submit_limit_order primitive instead. py_compile + import OK. NOT YET DEPLOYED — awaiting user restart/go. Reverts: FAVYES_MAX_DAILY_FIRES=50; EXIT099_ENABLED=False; _get_bankroll → drop the _free_usdc_cached() preface. Restarts 06-06 22:27 + 06-07 21:17 were both near European local midnight → yesterday's high frozen all next day → false lockouts from tick one. Bug was STILL LIVE on 06-08 (all 7 cities carrying 06-07 finals). FIX: when _prev==0 (post-restart), seed it from the local day the restored max belongs to (cs.running_max_ts) so a carried-over max RESETS instead of being suppressed. Self-heals on deploy restart. 2026-06-08 | SYSTEM/STWA-PRICER — A1 dew-correction bug QUANTIFIED (read-only backtest; no code change yet) | analysis/weather/dew_fix_crps_backtest.py | CONFIRMED + quantified the gap-analysis A1 finding. BUG (stwa_engine.py:665-669): humidity term reads self._nwp_cache (which holds AIR TEMP, line 556 hourly_temps) as `nwp_dew`, computing alpha*(dew_c − nwp_air_temp); but alpha was fit (stwa_fit_params.py:192-201) on the regressor (dew_c − dew_nwp_c) = obs dew − NWP DEW forecast. Mismatch = forecast dewpoint depression. BACKTEST (stwa_asos×stwa_nwp, n=669k hourly obs w/ full dew coverage, live fitted alpha+bias): hourly center MSE no-dew 1.336 → BUG 4.530 (+239%) → FIX 1.328; peak-window(±2h) 1.460 → BUG 3.757 (+157%) → FIX 1.438. Audit's "≈2× MSE" UNDERSTATED — bug TRIPLES hourly center MSE. Damage concentrated in dry/high-DPD cities (ankara MSE 2.1→26.4, jeddah 1.3→22.2, madrid 1.1→9.7, beijing 3.3→10.9); humid cities (singapore/KL/manila) ~unaffected. KEY CATCH: FIX (1.438) only −1.5% vs simply DISABLING the dew term (no-dew 1.460) → the correctly-wired dew correction adds ~nothing; the whole value is REMOVING the +239% injection. Also dew_nwp_c is 61.9% null (ecmwf_ifs025 leg; gfs has it) → a real fix only helps ~38% of rows, while the live bug fires on ALL cities (air-temp cache always present). RECOMMENDATION: disable the dew term (1-line, reversible) not plumb an NWP-dew cache — simplicity-first + live data says forecast skill barely drives NO WR (entry 613). NOT YET IMPLEMENTED (awaiting user go; bot is user-down). Memory: [[project_weather_gap_analysis]] [[project_stwa_engine]].

2026-06-08 | SYSTEM/STWA-STRATEGY — center→NO-PnL gate test: center accuracy is NOT the achievable NO lever; A3 uncertainty-gating is | analysis/weather/center_to_no_pnl.py (READ-ONLY) | Strategic gate after A1/A2: does fixed center → NO PnL? Structural test on 2021-24 ASOS×NWP w/ the now-fixed center (ensemble NWP max + per-(city,month) peak_bias) + deployed σ; favorite-longshot NO candidates = model p_NO∈[0.70,0.88] (mirrors live ask band); NO wins if realized max ∉ bucket; pseudo-EV at model-price + fixed 0.78. RESULT (n=45,244 candidates): NO WR by CENTER-ERROR quintile is U-SHAPED — err 0-0.27°C WR0.914 (+EV), 0.27-0.55 WR0.770, 0.55-0.92 WR0.653 (WORST,−EV), 0.92-1.51 WR0.721, 1.51-15°C WR0.941 (overshoot artifact: forecast catastrophically wrong → realized clears the shorted bucket → NO wins for wrong reason). corr(center_err,NO_win)=+0.096≈0 (confirms 613/610). KEY: in the ACHIEVABLE range (0-1.5°C) lower err → higher WR (so A1/A2 push the right way), BUT solid +EV needs center err <~0.3°C and our MEAN |center err|=0.81°C sits in the −EV zone; closing 0.8→0.3°C is ~2.5× beyond the NWP daily-max RMSE floor (~1°C). Overall WR=0.800 barely > 0.78 breakeven only because the Q1+Q5 tails rescue it; the typical day is −EV. CONCLUSION: further CENTER work (A4, conditional-isotonic center benefit, section-C center features) has LOW marginal NO value — can't reach the +EV accuracy zone. The real lever is the inverse: DON'T trade NO when we can't be accurate. Center err isn't observable at trade time → proxy = forecast UNCERTAINTY = A3 (revision-velocity→σ). High-revision/σ days = the moderate-error −EV zone; gating NO off there sidesteps the loss. A3 is the priority (not for added alpha, but to AVOID the structural −EV zone center accuracy can't escape). Caveats: no historical asks (model-price + fixed-0.78 sensitivity), no intraday x_hat, 2-model ensemble — mechanism test at n=45k, not live execution. Memory: [[project_weather_gap_analysis]] [[project_stwa_engine]] [[project_stwa_forecast_skill_measured]].

2026-06-08 | SYSTEM/STWA-STRATEGY — σ-gate for NO REFUTED (inverse); favorite-longshot forecast-NO is structurally marginal | analysis/weather/center_to_no_pnl.py | Tested whether deployed per-(city,month) σ (observable at trade time) gates the −EV NO zone. RESULT (same n=45k): OPPOSITE of a risk-gate — NO WR rises with σ: lowest-σ quintile (0.43-0.71) WR0.770 EV@0.78 −0.010 (the −EV slice), highest-σ (1.38-3.25) WR0.825 +0.045; corr(σ,NO_win)=+0.047. MECHANISM: candidate bucket sits at fixed PROB distance (p_no=0.78≈fixed z) → physical distance = z·σ; large σ → far bucket → common ~0.8°C center errors rarely reach it → NO wins; small σ → near bucket → errors land in it → NO loses. True predictor = center_err/σ (standardized miss), NOT observable at trade time. DEPLOYABLE crumb: a σ-FLOOR (skip NO on σ<~0.71 city-months) removes the −EV slice — but the whole effect is weak (all quintiles within ±0.045 of breakeven). BOTTOM LINE across both cuts: favorite-longshot forecast-NO is STRUCTURALLY MARGINAL at our ~0.8°C accuracy (aggregate WR 0.800 vs 0.78 breakeven = +0.02, rescued only by tails); neither center accuracy nor static σ gives a strong clean gate. REINFORCES that the durable edge is the calibration-FREE lockout (running-max floor), not forecast-NO; A1/A2 are correctness hygiene that do NOT unlock NO PnL. A3 revision-velocity (day-level center_err/σ proxy) remains the only untested shot at a gate — still n=8, data-gated. Memory: [[project_weather_gap_analysis]] [[project_stwa_forecast_skill_measured]] [[project_lockout_exec_timing]].
